### Universal Router on Robinhood Chain: resolved 2026-09-03

**Two UniversalRouter contracts are live on chain 4663, and this feed lists the one almost nobody calls.**
This was recorded as an unresolved discrepancy until 2026-09-03.
It is settled now, from the chain.
Full evidence, every step reproducible: [`_raw/refresh-2026-09-03/universal-router-evidence.txt`](_raw/refresh-2026-09-03/universal-router-evidence.txt).

#### Which to use

| If you are | Use | Why |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Swapping (v2, v3, v4), by hand or through the SDK | [`0x8876789976dEcBfCbBbe364623C63652db8C0904`](https://robinhoodchain.blockscout.com/address/0x8876789976dEcBfCbBbe364623C63652db8C0904) | What `universal-router-sdk`, the docs tables, the Trading API and 99.7 percent of live traffic use |
| Whitelisting a spender in a wallet or an app | [`0x8876789976dEcBfCbBbe364623C63652db8C0904`](https://robinhoodchain.blockscout.com/address/0x8876789976dEcBfCbBbe364623C63652db8C0904) | The Trading API's `swap.to` is always this address on 4663 |
| Using the Across cross-chain deposit command (`ChainedActions`) | [`0x06AfBA43Fd06227fA663b0DAecF536f6EaA6bf99`](https://robinhoodchain.blockscout.com/address/0x06AfBA43Fd06227fA663b0DAecF536f6EaA6bf99) | Only this one has a real SpokePool wired in; on the other, bridging is a stub |
| Reading a machine-readable Uniswap deployment feed | Expect `0x06AfBA43Fd06227fA663b0DAecF536f6EaA6bf99` | It is the only one `deployments.json` records, on any chain |

Neither address is deprecated and both were taking traffic within four minutes of each other on 2026-09-03.

#### The two contracts

| | `0x8876...0904` | `0x06Af...bf99` |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Deployed | block 18127, 2026-05-26 | block 3347899, 2026-07-06 |
| Deployer EOA | `0x9701fb0aDe1E269c8f64Ec0C7b3cfADB31A13A52` | same |
| Via | CREATE2 deployer `0x4e59b44847b379578588920cA78FbF26c0B4956C` | same |
| Runtime code | 24546 bytes | 24546 bytes |
| Runtime sha256 | `9b70267279e3d106d908cbd43927a251acd7a5e94cf34da431228c3c421c7dfb` | `01c1f00a112cdbd902431cf4dc3ea00aa8aeafc1cd0bd3347ebe66732988fb5b` |
| Verified sources (109 files) sha256 | `837f9921199617e1780369c554bce717c3714dc4f5f3f89ef5db7e5f308424d6` | same |
| `spokePool` immutable | `0x7332D11BD10d18A04B119Cd4671a96f3148002c4` (`UnsupportedProtocol`) | `0xD29C85F15DF544bA632C9E25829fd29d767d7978` (`ERC1967Proxy` over Across `Universal_SpokePool`) |
| Every other constructor parameter | permit2, weth9, v2Factory, v3Factory, pairInitCodeHash, poolInitCodeHash, v4PoolManager, v3NFTPositionManager, v4PositionManager | all identical |
| Transactions to 2026-09-03 | 16,933,489 | 46,028 |
| Distinct senders in the last 50 calls | 47 | 6 |
| In `deployments.json` | no | yes |
| In `universal-router-sdk` constants for 4663 | yes, as `V2_1_1`, `creationBlock` 18127 | no |
| Trading API `swap.to` on 4663 | yes | never observed |

#### Evidence chain

`eth_getCode` on chain 4663 returns 24546 bytes for both addresses, so both hold code and neither is a typo or a placeholder.
A byte-by-byte diff of the two runtime codes finds 111 differing bytes in six runs, and every one of the six is an immutable rather than logic: three copies of the `spokePool` address, the contract's own address, and the EIP-712 domain separator cached from that address.
The Blockscout verified sources are the same 109 files under the same compiler settings, hashing identically.
The decoded constructor arguments agree on every field except `spokePool`.
Grepping the verified sources shows `spokePool` reaches nothing but `ChainedActions.depositV3`, the Across cross-chain path, so it cannot affect any v2, v3 or v4 swap.

That explains the pair.
The `UnsupportedProtocol` stub was deployed at block 17815 on 2026-05-26 and the first router 312 blocks later the same day, before Across existed on this chain.
Across's `Universal_SpokePool` proxy arrived on 2026-06-23.
On 2026-07-06 the same deployer redeployed the same build with the real SpokePool address, and `deployments.json` (`generatedAt` 2026-07-15) recorded that one.
Uniswap's own swap tooling was never repointed, which is why the docs tables, the SDK constants and the live API all still name the 2026-05-26 deployment.

#### The Bags claim

The Bags docs describe `0x8876789976dEcBfCbBbe364623C63652db8C0904` as a Robinhood-modified fork of the Universal Router.
That is false.
Its verified sources hash identically to the other deployment's, and the only bytes that differ anywhere in its runtime code are three immutables.
There is no modification, no fork and no Robinhood-specific logic in it.
If anything it is the plainer of the two, because its bridging hook points at a revert stub.

#### Note on the archive's docs pages

`protocols/v4/deployments.md`, `protocols/v3/deployments/v3-robinhood-chain-deployments.md` and `trading/swapping-api/supported-chains.md` in this archive all list `0x8876789976dEcBfCbBbe364623C63652db8C0904` for chain 4663.
They are correct and have been left as captured.
The table above is generated from `deployments.json` and is also correct; the feed simply records a different deployment than the docs do.
